range(len(A))]\n\n\ndef scale_matrix(c: complex, A: Matrix) -> Matrix:\n    return [[c * x for x in row] for row in A]\n\n\ndef trace_matrix(A: Matrix) -> complex:\n    n = len(A)\n    return sum((A[i][i] for i in range(n)), 0j)\n\n\ndef frobenius_norm(A: Matrix) -> float:\n    s = 0.0\n    for row in A:\n        for x in row:\n            ax = abs(x)\n            s += ax * ax\n    return math.sqrt(s)\n\n\ndef commutator_norm(A: Matrix, B: Matrix) -> float:\n    return frobenius_norm(sub_matrices(matmul(A, B), matmul(B, A)))\n\n\ndef is_square(A: Matrix) -> bool:\n    n = len(A)\n    return all(len(row) == n for row in A)\n\n\ndef is_hermitian(A: Matrix, tol: float = 1e-10) -> bool:\n    if not is_square(A):\n        return False\n    n = len(A)\n    for i in range(n):\n        if abs(A[i][i].imag) > tol:\n            return False\n        for j in range(i + 1, n):\n            if abs(A[i][j] - A[j][i].conjugate()) > tol:\n                return False\n    return True\n\n\ndef is_projector(P: Matrix, tol: float = 1e-10) -> bool:\n    if not is_hermitian(P, tol=tol):\n        return False\n    return frobenius_norm(sub_matrices(matmul(P, P), P)) <= tol\n\n\ndef outer_product(v: Sequence[complex]) -> Matrix:\n    n = len(v)\n    P = zero_matrix(n, n)\n    for i in range(n):\n        vi = complex(v[i])\n        for j in range(n):\n            P[i][j] = vi * complex(v[j]).conjugate()\n    return P\n\n\ndef vector_inner(u: Sequence[complex], v: Sequence[complex]) -> complex:\n    if len(u) != len(v):\n        raise ValueError(\"vector_inner dimension mismatch\")\n    return sum((complex(u[i]).conjugate() * complex(v[i]) for i in range(len(u))), 0j)\n\n\ndef vector_norm(v: Sequence[complex]) -> float:\n    return math.sqrt(max(0.0, vector_inner(v, v).real))\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 8. 3x3 inverse: two independent implementations for cross-check.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef inv3_gauss(A: Matrix) -> Matrix:\n    \"\"\"Gauss-Jordan inverse for 3x3 complex matrix.\"\"\"\n    A = _as_complex_matrix(A)\n    M = [\n        [A[i][0], A[i][1], A[i][2], 0j, 0j, 0j]\n        for i in range(3)\n    ]\n    M[0][3] = 1 + 0j\n    M[1][4] = 1 + 0j\n    M[2][5] = 1 + 0j\n\n    for col in range(3):\n        pivot_row = max(range(col, 3), key=lambda r: abs(M[r][col]))\n        if abs(M[pivot_row][col]) < 1e-14:\n            raise ZeroDivisionError(\"singular 3x3 matrix\")\n\n        if pivot_row != col:\n            M[col], M[pivot_row] = M[pivot_row], M[col]\n\n        pivot = M[col][col]\n        for j in range(6):\n            M[col][j] /= pivot\n\n        for r in range(3):\n            if r == col:\n                continue\n            factor = M[r][col]\n            if abs(factor) > 0.0:\n                for j in range(6):\n                    M[r][j] -= factor * M[col][j]\n\n    return [[M[i][3 + j] for j in range(3)] for i in range(3)]\n\n\ndef det3(A: Matrix) -> complex:\n    a, b, c = A[0][0], A[0][1], A[0][2]\n    d, e, f = A[1][0], A[1][1], A[1][2]\n    g, h, i = A[2][0], A[2][1], A[2][2]\n    return a * (e * i - f * h) - b * (d * i - f * g) + c * (d * h - e * g)\n\n\ndef inv3_adjugate(A: Matrix) -> Matrix:\n    \"\"\"Adjugate/determinant inverse for 3x3 complex matrix.\"\"\"\n    A = _as_complex_matrix(A)\n    det = det3(A)\n    if abs(det) < 1e-14:\n        raise ZeroDivisionError(\"singular 3x3 matrix\")\n\n    a, b, c = A[0][0], A[0][1], A[0][2]\n    d, e, f = A[1][0], A[1][1], A[1][2]\n    g, h, i = A[2][0], A[2][1], A[2][2]\n\n    C00 = e * i - f * h\n    C01 = -(d * i - f * g)\n    C02 = d * h - e * g\n\n    C10 = -(b * i - c * h)\n    C11 = a * i - c * g\n    C12 = -(a * h - b * g)\n\n    C20 = b * f - c * e\n    C21 = -(a * f - c * d)\n    C22 = a * e - b * d\n\n    inv = [\n        [C00 / det, C10 / det, C20 / det],\n        [C01 / det, C11 / det, C21 / det],\n        [C02 / det, C12 / det, C22 / det],\n    ]\n    return inv\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 9. Hermitian eigenvalues.\n#    Uses numpy if available; otherwise pure-Python real symmetric embedding.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef _jacobi_real_symmetric_eigenvalues(\n    A: Sequence[Sequence[float]],\n    max_sweeps: int = 200,\n    tol: float = 1e-12,\n) -> List[float]:\n    n = len(A)\n    if n == 0:\n        return []\n\n    M = [list(map(float, row)) for row in A]\n\n    for _ in range(max_sweeps):\n        off = 0.0\n        for i in range(n):\n            for j in range(i + 1, n):\n                off += M[i][j] * M[i][j]\n\n        if off <= tol * tol:\n            break\n\n        for p in range(n - 1):\n            for q in range(p + 1, n):\n                apq = M[p][q]\n                if abs(apq) <= 1e-300:\n                    continue\n\n                app = M[p][p]\n                aqq = M[q][q]\n\n                if abs(app - aqq) <= 1e-300:\n                    theta = 1.0\n                else:\n                    theta = (aqq - app) / (2.0 * apq)\n\n                if theta == 0.0:\n                    t = 1.0\n                else:\n                    t = 1.0 / (theta + math.copysign(math.sqrt(theta * theta + 1.0), theta))\n\n                c = 1.0 / math.sqrt(t * t + 1.0)\n                s = t * c\n\n                for i in range(n):\n                    if i == p or i == q:\n                        continue\n\n                    mip = M[i][p]\n                    miq = M[i][q]\n\n                    M[i][p] = c * mip - s * miq\n                    M[p][i] = M[i][p]\n\n                    M[i][q] = s * mip + c * miq\n                    M[q][i] = M[i][q]\n\n                mpp = M[p][p]\n                mqq = M[q][q]\n                mpq = M[p][q]\n\n                M[p][p] = c * c * mpp - 2.0 * s * c * mpq + s * s * mqq\n                M[q][q] = s * s * mpp + 2.0 * s * c * mpq + c * c * mqq\n                M[p][q] = 0.0\n                M[q][p] = 0.0\n\n    eigs = [float(M[i][i]) for i in range(n)]\n    eigs.sort(reverse=True)\n    return eigs\n\n\ndef _hermitian_to_real_embedding(H: Matrix) -> List[List[float]]:\n    n = len(H)\n    R = [[0.0 for _ in range(2 * n)] for _ in range(2 * n)]\n\n    for i in range(n):\n        for j in range(n):\n            z = H[i][j]\n            a = float(z.real)\n            b = float(z.imag)\n\n            R[i][j] = a\n            R[n + i][n + j] = a\n\n            R[i][n + j] = -b\n            R[n + i][j] = b\n\n    return R\n\n\ndef hermitian_eigenvalues(H: Matrix) -> Tuple[List[float], int, float, float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Returns:\n        eigenvalues descending,\n        rank,\n        lambda_max,\n        lambda_min clamped to zero if tiny negative/rank-deficient.\n    \"\"\"\n    n = len(H)\n    if n == 0:\n        return [], 0, 0.0, 0.0\n\n    if not is_hermitian(H, tol=1e-9):\n        raise ValueError(\"hermitian_eigenvalues requires a Hermitian matrix\")\n\n    if HAVE_NUMPY:\n        arr = np.array([[complex(H[i][j]) for j in range(n)] for i in range(n)], dtype=complex)\n        vals_np = np.linalg.eigvalsh(arr)\n        vals = [float(x) for x in vals_np]\n        vals.sort(reverse=True)\n        vals = [0.0 if (-1e-9 < x < 0.0) else x for x in vals]\n        rank = sum(1 for x in vals if x > 1e-10)\n        lam_max = vals[0] if vals else 0.0\n        lam_min = vals[-1] if vals else 0.0\n    else:\n        R = _hermitian_to_real_embedding(H)\n        raw = _jacobi_real_symmetric_eigenvalues(R)\n        raw = [0.0 if (-1e-9 < x < 0.0) else x for x in raw]\n\n        positive_count = sum(1 for x in raw if x > 1e-10)\n        rank = max(0, min(n, int(round(positive_count / 2.0))))\n\n        lam_max = max(raw) if raw else 0.0\n        lam_min = min(raw) if raw else 0.0\n\n        vals: List[float] = []\n        for x in raw:\n            if not vals or abs(x - vals[-1]) > 1e-8:\n                vals.append(float(x))\n            else:\n                vals[-1] = 0.5 * (vals[-1] + float(x))\n\n    if rank < n and lam_min < 1e-8:\n        lam_min = 0.0\n\n    return vals, rank, lam_max, lam_min\n\n\ndef access_metrics(L: Matrix) -> Tuple[Matrix, List[float], int, float, float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    M_C = L^dagger L.\n\n    Returns:\n        M_C,\n        eigenvalues descending,\n        rank,\n        kappa_C = lambda_min / lambda_max,\n        p_bar = Tr(M_C) / d_V.\n    \"\"\"\n    M = matmul(conj_transpose(L), L)\n    d_V = len(M)\n\n    eigs, rank, lam_max, lam_min = hermitian_eigenvalues(M)\n\n    if lam_max <= 1e-12:\n        kappa = 0.0\n    else:\n        kappa = max(0.0, lam_min) / lam_max\n\n    p_bar = trace_matrix(M).real / float(d_V)\n\n    return M, eigs, rank, kappa, p_bar\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 10. mu_V operator blocks and Sigma code-consistency.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef mu_operator_blocks(V: Matrix, energies: Sequence[float]) -> Dict[float, Matrix]:\n    if not V:\n        raise ValueError(\"V must be non-empty\")\n\n    d_C = len(V[0])\n    blocks: Dict[float, Matrix] = {}\n\n    for row, E in zip(V, energies):\n        if len(row) != d_C:\n            raise ValueError(\"All rows of V must have channel dimension d_C\")\n\n        key = float(E)\n        B = blocks.get(key, zero_matrix(d_C, d_C))\n\n        for a in range(d_C):\n            ra = complex(row[a])\n            for b in range(d_C):\n                B[a][b] += ra.conjugate() * complex(row[b])\n\n        blocks[key] = B\n\n    return blocks\n\n\ndef sigma_from_mu_blocks(blocks: Dict[float, Matrix], z: complex) -> Matrix:\n    if not blocks:\n        raise ValueError(\"empty mu_V blocks\")\n\n    d_C = len(next(iter(blocks.values())))\n    S = zero_matrix(d_C, d_C)\n\n    for E, B in blocks.items():\n        factor = 1.0 / (z - complex(E))\n        S = add_matrices(S, scale_matrix(factor, B))\n\n    return S\n\n\ndef direct_sigma_from_V(V: Matrix, energies: Sequence[float], z: complex) -> Matrix:\n    if not V:\n        raise ValueError(\"V must be non-empty\")\n\n    d_C = len(V[0])\n    S = zero_matrix(d_C, d_C)\n\n    for row, E in zip(V, energies):\n        factor = 1.0 / (z - complex(E))\n        for a in range(d_C):\n            ra = complex(row[a])\n            for b in range(d_C):\n                S[a][b] += ra.conjugate() * complex(row[b]) * factor\n\n    return S\n\n\ndef trace_blocks_equal(\n    blocks_a: Dict[float, Matrix],\n    blocks_b: Dict[float, Matrix],\n    tol: float = 1e-10,\n) -> bool:\n    if set(blocks_a.keys()) != set(blocks_b.keys()):\n        return False\n\n    for key in blocks_a:\n        ta = trace_matrix(blocks_a[key])\n        tb = trace_matrix(blocks_b[key])\n        if abs(ta - tb) > tol:\n            return False\n\n    return True\n\n\ndef operator_blocks_differ(\n    blocks_a: Dict[float, Matrix],\n    blocks_b: Dict[float, Matrix],\n    threshold: float = 1e-1,\n) -> bool:\n    if set(blocks_a.keys()) != set(blocks_b.keys()):\n        return True\n\n    for key in blocks_a:\n        diff = frobenius_norm(sub_matrices(blocks_a[key], blocks_b[key]))\n        if diff > threshold:\n            return True\n\n    return False\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 11. Casimir / zero-point conventions.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef zero_point_energy_from_angular_frequencies(\n    arch: Architecture,\n    omegas: Sequence[float],\n) -> float:\n    total = 0.0\n    for w in omegas:\n        wf = float(w)\n        if not math.isfinite(wf) or wf < 0.0:\n            raise ValueError(\"omega must be finite and non-negative\")\n        total += wf\n\n    half = arch.zero_point_half.unwrap(ConstantTag.ZERO_POINT_HALF)\n    return half * float(arch.hbar) * total\n\n\ndef zero_point_energy_from_mode_energies_hbar_omega(\n    arch: Architecture,\n    mode_energies: Sequence[float],\n) -> float:\n    total = 0.0\n    for e in mode_energies:\n        ef = float(e)\n        if not math.isfinite(ef) or ef < 0.0:\n            raise ValueError(\"hbar*omega mode energy must be finite and non-negative\")\n        total += ef\n\n    half = arch.zero_point_half.unwrap(ConstantTag.ZERO_POINT_HALF)\n    return half * total\n\n\ndef zero_point_energy_from_mode_energies_hbar_omega_over_2(\n    arch: Architecture,\n    half_quanta: Sequence[float],\n) -> float:\n    total = 0.0\n    for e in half_quanta:\n        ef = float(e)\n        if not math.isfinite(ef) or ef < 0.0:\n            raise ValueError(\"hbar*omega/2 value must be finite and non-negative\")\n        total += ef\n\n    return total\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 12. Three-level E_merge model.\n#     This is the finite model skeleton used for internal pipeline validation.\n#     It is NOT a corpus replicator.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nDEFAULT_EV = 0.0\nDEFAULT_Z = 2.0 + 0.1j\n\n\ndef observables(\n    w: Sequence[float],\n    z: complex = DEFAULT_Z,\n    EV: float = DEFAULT_EV,\n) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    w = (gamma, t1, t2, E1, E2)\n\n    Returns:\n        kappa = |gamma t1 t2|\n        Delta = |gamma - t1 t2 / (z - E1)|\n        p     = |G_02|^2, G = (H - zI)^{-1}\n\n    Safe against singular denominators/matrix inversion by returning large\n    sentinel values instead of raising inside optimizer evaluations.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        gamma = float(w[0])\n        t1 = float(w[1])\n        t2 = float(w[2])\n        E1 = float(w[3])\n        E2 = float(w[4])\n    except Exception:\n        return SAFE_BIG, SAFE_BIG, 0.0\n\n    if not all(math.isfinite(x) for x in (gamma, t1, t2, E1, E2)):\n        return SAFE_BIG, SAFE_BIG, 0.0\n\n    denom = complex(z) - complex(E1)\n    if abs(denom) < 1e-14:\n        Delta = SAFE_BIG\n    else:\n        Delta = abs(complex(gamma) - complex(t1) * complex(t2) / denom)\n\n    kappa = abs(complex(gamma) * complex(t1) * complex(t2))\n\n    H = [\n        [complex(EV), complex(t1), complex(gamma)],\n        [complex(t1), complex(E1), complex(t2)],\n        [complex(gamma), complex(t2), complex(E2)],\n    ]\n\n    M = [\n        [H[0][0] - complex(z), H[0][1], H[0][2]],\n        [H[1][0], H[1][1] - complex(z), H[1][2]],\n        [H[2][0], H[2][1], H[2][2] - complex(z)],\n    ]\n\n    try:\n        G = inv3_gauss(M)\n        p = abs(G[0][2]) ** 2\n    except ZeroDivisionError:\n        p = 0.0\n\n    if not math.isfinite(kappa):\n        kappa = SAFE_BIG\n    if not math.isfinite(Delta):\n        Delta = SAFE_BIG\n    if not math.isfinite(p):\n        p = 0.0\n\n    return float(kappa), float(Delta), float(p)\n\n\ndef metric_diag(w0: Sequence[float], kind: str) -> List[float]:\n    if kind == \"abs\":\n        return [1.0 for _ in range(5)]\n\n    if kind == \"rel\":\n        out = []\n        for x in w0:\n            ax = abs(float(x))\n            if not math.isfinite(ax) or ax < 1e-12:\n                ax = 1e-12\n            out.append(1.0 / (ax * ax))\n        return out\n\n    raise ValueError(f\"unknown metric kind: {kind}\")\n\n\ndef weighted_norm2(dw: Sequence[float], metric: Sequence[float]) -> float:\n    s = 0.0\n    for m, x in zip(metric, dw):\n        xf = float(x)\n        mf = float(m)\n        if not math.isfinite(xf) or not math.isfinite(mf):\n            return SAFE_BIG\n        s += mf * xf * xf\n    return float(s)\n\n\ndef synthetic_w0(gamma: float) -> List[float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Frozen synthetic 3-level base point for internal E_merge cross-tests.\n\n    This is NOT the corpus parameterization.\n    It is only used to validate the optimization/audit pipeline.\n    \"\"\"\n    return [float(gamma), 1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.5]\n\n\ndef emerge_scipy(\n    w0: Sequence[float],\n    metric_kind: str = \"abs\",\n    delta_floor: float = 0.05,\n    kappa_floor: float = 0.5,\n    use_p_constraint: bool = False,\n    start: Optional[Sequence[float]] = None,\n    z: complex = DEFAULT_Z,\n    EV: float = DEFAULT_EV,\n) -> Tuple[float, Any, Dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Constrained minimization:\n        min ||dw||_g^2\n        s.t. Delta(w0+dw) <= delta_floor\n             kappa(w0+dw) >= kappa_floor * kappa0\n             optionally p(w0+dw) >= p0\n\n    Requires scipy/numpy.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not (HAVE_SCIPY and HAVE_NUMPY):\n        raise RuntimeError(\"scipy/numpy required for emerge_scipy\")\n\n    w0_list = [float(x) for x in w0]\n    w0_np = np.asarray(w0_list, dtype=float)\n\n    kappa0, Delta0, p0 = observables(w0_list, z=z, EV=EV)\n\n    if Delta0 <= delta_floor + 1e-12:\n        return 0.0, w0_np.copy(), {\n            \"initial_feasible\": True,\n            \"success\": True,\n            \"status\": 0,\n            \"message\": \"initial_feasible\",\n            \"kappa0\": kappa0,\n            \"Delta0\": Delta0,\n            \"p0\": p0,\n            \"objective\": 0.0,\n            \"metric_kind\": metric_kind,\n            \"delta_floor\": delta_floor,\n            \"kappa_floor\": kappa_floor,\n            \"use_p_constraint\": use_p_constraint,\n        }\n\n    metric = np.asarray(metric_diag(w0_list, metric_kind), dtype=float)\n    threshold = float(kappa_floor) * float(kappa0)\n\n    def obj(dw: Any) -> float:\n        dw = np.asarray(dw, dtype=float)\n        val = float(np.sum(metric * dw * dw))\n        return val if math.isfinite(val) else SAFE_BIG\n\n    def c_delta(dw: Any) -> float:\n        dw = np.asarray(dw, dtype=float)\n        _, Delta, _ = observables((w0_np + dw).tolist(), z=z, EV=EV)\n        val = float(delta_floor - Delta)\n        if not math.isfinite(val):\n            return -SAFE_BIG\n        return val\n\n    def c_kappa(dw: Any) -> float:\n        dw = np.asarray(dw, dtype=float)\n        kappa, _, _ = observables((w0_np + dw).tolist(), z=z, EV=EV)\n        val = float(kappa - threshold)\n        if not math.isfinite(val):\n            return -SAFE_BIG\n        return val\n\n    cons = [\n        {\"type\": \"ineq\", \"fun\": c_delta},\n        {\"type\": \"ineq\", \"fun\": c_kappa},\n    ]\n\n    if use_p_constraint:\n        def c_p(dw: Any) -> float:\n            dw = np.asarray(dw, dtype=float)\n            _, _, p = observables((w0_np + dw).tolist(), z=z, EV=EV)\n            val = float(p - p0)\n            if not math.isfinite(val):\n                return -SAFE_BIG\n            return val\n\n        cons.append({\"type\": \"ineq\", \"fun\": c_p})\n\n    x0 = np.zeros(5, dtype=float) if start is None else np.asarray(start, dtype=float)\n\n    res = minimize(\n        obj,\n        x0,\n        constraints=cons,\n        method=\"SLSQP\",\n        options={\"maxiter\": 1000, \"ftol\": 1e-12, \"disp\": False},\n    )\n\n    w_opt = w0_np + np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)\n    kappa_opt, Delta_opt, p_opt = observables(w_opt.tolist(), z=z, EV=EV)\n\n    cost = float(res.fun)\n    if not math.isfinite(cost):\n        cost = SAFE_BIG\n\n    info = {\n        \"initial_feasible\": False,\n        \"success\": bool(res.success),\n        \"status\": int(res.status),\n        \"message\": str(res.message),\n        \"kappa0\": kappa0,\n        \"Delta0\": Delta0,\n        \"p0\": p0,\n        \"kappa_opt\": kappa_opt,\n        \"Delta_opt\": Delta_opt,\n        \"p_opt\": p_opt,\n        \"objective\": cost,\n        \"metric_kind\": metric_kind,\n        \"delta_floor\": delta_floor,\n        \"kappa_floor\": kappa_floor,\n        \"use_p_constraint\": use_p_constraint,\n    }\n\n    return cost, w_opt, info\n\n\ndef _verify_emerge_candidate(\n    w0: Sequence[float],\n    w_opt: Any,\n    metric_kind: str,\n    delta_floor: float,\n    kappa_floor: float,\n    kappa0: float,\n    cost: float,\n) -> Tuple[bool, Dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Independent verification of an optimizer candidate.\n\n    A candidate is accepted only if:\n      - constraints are satisfied within tolerance;\n      - objective can be recomputed from dw and metric;\n      - recomputed objective matches reported objective.\n    \"\"\"\n    w0_list = [float(x) for x in w0]\n    w_opt_list = [float(x) for x in w_opt]\n\n    kappa_opt, Delta_opt, p_opt = observables(w_opt_list)\n    dw = [w_opt_list[i] - w0_list[i] for i in range(5)]\n    metric = metric_diag(w0_list, metric_kind)\n    recomputed = weighted_norm2(dw, metric)\n\n    cons_tol = 1e-6\n    feasible = (\n        Delta_opt <= delta_floor + cons_tol\n        and kappa_opt >= kappa_floor * kappa0 - cons_tol\n    )\n\n    cost_tol = 1e-6 + 1e-5 * max(1.0, abs(cost))\n    cost_ok = (\n        math.isfinite(recomputed)\n        and recomputed >= -1e-8\n        and abs(recomputed - cost) <= cost_tol\n    )\n\n    details = {\n        \"feasible\": bool(feasible),\n        \"cost_ok\": bool(cost_ok),\n        \"Delta_opt\": Delta_opt,\n        \"kappa_opt\": kappa_opt,\n        \"p_opt\": p_opt,\n        \"reported_cost\": cost,\n        \"recomputed_cost\": recomputed,\n        \"cost_tol\": cost_tol,\n    }\n\n    return bool(feasible and cost_ok), details\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 13. Corpus anchors from Цена границы.txt.\n#     These are reference targets, not reproduced by default.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nCORPUS_EMERGE_ANCHORS_DELTA_09_ABS: Dict[str, Any] = {\n    \"source\": \"Цена границы.txt, slice Delta_hold ~= 0.9, absolute metric\",\n    \"gamma\": [0.27, 0.45, 0.60, 0.75, 0.95],\n    \"E_abs\": [0.0423, 0.0241, 0.0154, 0.0101, 0.0060],\n    \"monotonicity\": {\n        \"dE_dgamma\": \"negative_on_grid\",\n        \"violations_abs\": 0,\n        \"violations_rel\": 0,\n        \"grid_size\": 56,\n    },\n    \"endpoint_ratio_abs\": 0.0423 / 0.0060,\n    \"status\": \"corpus_anchor_not_replicated_in_repo_by_default\",\n}\n\nCORPUS_EMERGE_SECONDARY_CURVE: Dict[str, Any] = {\n    \"source\": \"Цена границы.txt, fragment table\",\n    \"gamma\": [\n        0.332, 0.394, 0.455, 0.517, 0.579,\n        0.641, 0.703, 0.765, 0.826, 0.888, 0.950,\n    ],\n    \"E_abs\": [\n        0.0348, 0.0287, 0.0237, 0.0197, 0.0164,\n        0.0137, 0.0115, 0.0097, 0.0082, 0.0070, 0.0060,\n    ],\n    \"status\": \"corpus_anchor_fragment_not_replicated_in_repo_by_default\",\n}\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 14. Corpus replicator hook.\n#     To make strict PASS possible, a validated replicator must be registered.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nCorpusReplicator = Callable[[Sequence[float], str], Dict[str, Any]]\n\nCORPUS_REPLICATOR: Optional[CorpusReplicator] = None\n\n# Contract for a future replicator:\n#\n# def my_replicator(gammas: Sequence[float], metric_kind: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n#     return {\n#         \"E\": [...],\n#         \"method\": \"three_level_slqp_from_muV_or_validated_reduction\",\n#         \"parameters\": {...},\n#         \"independent_checks\": {...},\n#         \"provenance\": \"...\",\n#     }\n#\n# CORPUS_REPLICATOR = my_replicator\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 15. Check helpers.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef _check(cond: bool, msg: str = \"check failed\") -> None:\n    if not cond:\n        raise AssertionError(msg)\n\n\ndef _close(a: float, b: float, tol: float = 1e-9) -> bool:\n    return abs(float(a) - float(b)) <= tol\n\n\ndef _expect_raises(exc: type, fn: Callable[[], Any], msg: str = \"expected exception\") -> bool:\n    try:\n        fn()\n    except exc:\n        return True\n    except Exception as e:\n        raise AssertionError(f\"{msg}: unexpected exception {type(e).__name__}: {e}\") from e\n    raise AssertionError(msg)\n\n\ndef _fmt_complex(z: complex) -> str:\n    return f\"{z.real:+.8f}{z.imag:+.8f}j\"\n\n\ndef _matrix_summary(A: Matrix) -> str:\n    rows = []\n    for row in A:\n        rows.append(\"[\" + \", \".join(_fmt_complex(x) for x in row) + \"]\")\n    return \"\\n  \" + \"\\n  \".join(rows)\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 16. Gate: typed core and original eight fixes.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef gate_typed_core() -> GateResult:\n    warnings: List[str] = []\n\n    # Fix 1: outcomes give lower bound, not equality.\n    _check(minimum_channel_dimension_for_outcomes(1) == 1)\n    _check(minimum_channel_dimension_for_outcomes(2) == 2)\n    _check(minimum_channel_dimension_for_outcomes(3) == 4)\n    _check(minimum_channel_dimension_for_outcomes(4) == 4)\n    _check(minimum_qubit_count_for_outcomes(2) == 1)\n    _check(minimum_qubit_count_for_outcomes(3) == 2)\n    _check(derive_d_V_lower_bound_from_outcomes((\"CONT\", \"RESTART\")) == 2)\n\n    # Gate: inverse cross-check.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef gate_inverse_cross_check() -> GateResult:\n    A = _as_complex_matrix([\n        [2.0 + 0.1j, 1.0, 0.0],\n        [1.0, 3.0 - 0.2j, 1.0],\n        [0.0, 1.0, 2.0 + 0.05j],\n    ])\n\n    inv_g = inv3_gauss(A)\n    inv_a = inv3_adjugate(A)\n\n    err = frobenius_norm(sub_matrices(inv_g, inv_a))\n    _check(err < 1e-12, f\"inverse cross-check failed: err={err}\")\n\n    I = identity_matrix(3)\n    prod_err = frobenius_norm(sub_matrices(matmul(A, inv_g), I))\n    _check(prod_err < 1e-12, f\"A*inv(A) != I: err={prod_err}\")\n\n    return GateResult(\n        name=\"inverse_cross_check\",\n        passed=True,\n        status=\"PASS\",\n        details={\n            \"gauss_vs_adjugate_frobenius\": err,\n            \"A_times_inv_minus_I_frobenius\": prod_err,\n        },\n    )\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 18. Gate: isospectral operator orientation.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef gate_isospectral_operator_orientation() -> GateResult:\n    energies_A = \"golden_sigma_diff\": golden_sigma_diff,\n        },\n    )\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 19. Gate: mu_V -> Sigma code consistency only.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef gate_mu_sigma_code_consistency() -> GateResult:\n    energies = [0.0, 1.0]\n    z = 0.3 + 0.4j\n    inv_sqrt2 = 1.0 / math.sqrt(2.0)\n\n    V_A = _as_complex_matrix([\n        [1.0, 0.0],\n        [0.0, 1.0],\n    ])\n\n    V_B = _as_complex_matrix([\n        [inv_sqrt2,  inv_sqrt2],\n        [inv_sqrt2, -inv_sqrt2],\n    ])\n\n    blocks_A = mu_operator_blocks(V_A, energies)\n    blocks_B = mu_operator_blocks(V_B, energies)\n\n    sigma_direct_A = direct_sigma_from_V(V_A, energies, z)\n    sigma_mu_A = sigma_from_mu_blocks(blocks_A, z)\n\n    sigma_direct_B = direct_sigma_from_V(V_B, energies, z)\n    sigma_mu_B = sigma_from_mu_blocks(blocks_B, z)\n\n    err_A = frobenius_norm(sub_matrices(sigma_direct_A, sigma_mu_A))\n    err_B = frobenius_norm(sub_matrices(sigma_direct_B, sigma_mu_B))\n\n    _check(err_A < 1e-12, f\"code consistency A failed: err={err_A}\")\n    _check(err_B < 1e-12, f\"code consistency B failed: err={err_B}\")\n\n    return GateResult(\n        name=\"mu_sigma_code_consistency\",\n        passed=True,\n        status=FINAL_STATUS[\"mu_sigma_code_consistency\"],\n        details={\n            \"oracle_error_A\": err_A,\n            \"oracle_error_B\": err_B,\n            \"note\": (\n                \"This checks that two algebraically identical implementations \"\n                \"of the same finite sum agree. It is not a physical oracle.\"\n            ),\n        },\n    )\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 20. Gate: dark stress, with fixed unpacking and analytic pins.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef _toy_dark_H0() -> Matrix:\n    return _as_complex_matrix([\n        [0.7, 0.0, 0.0],\n        [0.0, 1.0, 0.25],\n        [0.0, 0.25, 2.0],\n    ])\n\n\ndef _toy_dark_W0dag() -> Matrix:\n    return _as_complex_matrix([\n        [1.0, 0.0, 0.0],\n    ])\n\n\ndef _add_bright_dark_coupling(H: Matrix, eps: float) -> Matrix:\n    Hp = [row[:] for row in H]\n    Hp[0][1] += complex(eps)\n    Hp[1][0] += complex(eps)\n    return Hp\n\n\ndef _add_channel_dark_component(Wdag: Matrix, alpha: float) -> Matrix:\n    Wp = [row[:] for row in Wdag]\n    Wp[0][1] += complex(alpha)\n    return Wp\n\n\ndef gate_dark_stress_fixed() -> GateResult:\n    H0 = _toy_dark_H0()\n    W0dag = _toy_dark_W0dag()\n\n    # True dark projector for block-diagonal input.\n    P_D0 = diag_matrix([0.0, 1.0, 1.0])\n\n    _check(is_projector(P_D0), \"P_D0 is not a projector\")\n    _check(commutator_norm(H0, P_D0) < 1e-12, \"P_D0 should commute with H0\")\n    _check(frobenius_norm(matmul(W0dag, P_D0)) < 1e-12, \"P_D0 should be in ker W0^dagger\")\n\n    eps_values = [0.0, 0.01, 0.1, 0.3]\n    bright_dark_rows: List[Tuple[float, float]] = []\n\n    for eps in eps_values:\n        Heps = _add_bright_dark_coupling(H0, eps)\n        viol = commutator_norm(Heps, P_D0)\n        analytic = math.sqrt(2.0) * abs(eps)\n        bright_dark_rows.append((eps, viol))\n\n        _check(\n            math.isclose(viol, analytic, rel_tol=1e-12, invariance\")\n        else:\n            _check(viol > 1e-8, \"eps>0 should break invariance\")\n\n    stress_want = [\n        0.0,\n        0.01414213562373095,\n        0.14142135623730953,\n        0.4242640687119285,\n    ]\n    _check(len(bright_dark_rows) == len(stress_want))\n    for idx, (eps, got) in enumerate(bright_dark_rows):\n        want = stress_want[idx]\n        _check(\n            math.isclose(got, want, rel_tol=1e-12, abs_tol=1e-12),\n            f\"dark stress pin drift eps={eps}: got={got}, want={want}\",\n        )\n\n    # Channel dark component stress.\n    alpha_values = [0.0, 0.01, 0.1, 1.0 / math.sqrt(2.0)]\n    channel_rows: List[Tuple[float, float, float]] = []\n\n    for alpha in alpha_values:\n        Walpha = _add_channel_dark_component(W0dag, alpha)\n        old_kernel_violation = frobenius_norm(matmul(Walpha, P_D0))\n\n        # Recompute a 2D kernel projector for Walpha = [1, alpha, 0].\n        wrow = [complex(x) for x in Walpha[0]]\n        basis: List[List[complex]] = []\n\n        for e in [\n            [1.0 + 0j, 0.0 + 0j, 0.0 + 0j],\n            [0.0 + 0j, 1.0 + 0j, 0.0 + 0j],\n            [0.0 + 0j, 0.0 + 0j, 1.0 + 0j],\n        ]:\n            v = e[:]\n            wnorm2 = vector_inner(wrow, wrow).real\n            if wnorm2 <= 1e-14:\n                proj = v\n            else:\n                coeff = vector_inner(wrow, v) / wnorm2\n                proj = [v[i] - coeff * wrow[i] for i in range(3)]\n\n            for q in basis:\n                c = vector_inner(q, proj)\n                proj = [proj[i] - c * q[i] for i in range(3)]\n\n            nrm = vector_norm(proj)\n            if nrm > 1e-10:\n                basis.append([x / nrm for x in proj])\n\n            if len(basis) == 2:\n                break\n\n        _check(len(basis) == 2, \"failed to build 2D kernel basis\")\n\n        P_new = zero_matrix(3, 3)\n        for q in basis:\n            for i in range(3):\n                qi = q[i]\n                for j in range(3):\n                    P_new[i][j] += qi * q[j].conjugate()\n\n        new_kernel_error = frobenius_norm(matmul(Walpha, P_new))\n        new_commutator_with_H0 = \"dark_existence\": FINAL_STATUS[\"dark_existence\"],\n            \"bic_continuum_status\": FINAL_STATUS[\"bic_continuum_quadratic_width\"],\n            \"note\": (\n                \"Finite -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 21. Gate: top-k fake dark control.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef gate_topk_fake_dark_control() -> GateResult:\n    H = _toy_dark_H0()\n    Wdag = _toy_dark_W0dag()\n\n    P_fake_bright = diag_matrix([1.0, 0.0, 0.0])\n    P_fake_dark_coord = diag_matrix([0.0, 1.0, 0.0])\n\n    bright_kernel_violation = frobenius_norm(matmul(Wdag, P_fake_bright))\n    dark_coord_kernel_error = frobenius_norm(matmul(Wdag, P_fake_dark_coord))\n    dark_coord_commutator_violation = commutator_norm(H, P_fake_dark_coord)\n\n    _check(bright_kernel_violation > 1e-8, \"fake bright must fail kernel\")\n    _check(dark_coord_kernel_error < 1e-12, \"fake dark coordinate may pass kernel\")\n    _check(dark_coord_commutator_violation > 1e-8, \"fake dark coordinate must fail invariance\")\n\n    return GateResult(\n        name=\"topk_fake_dark_control\",\n        passed=True,\n        status=FINAL_STATUS[\"topk_fake_dark_control\"],\n        details={\n            \"bright_kernel_violation\": bright_kernel_violation,\n            \"dark_coord_kernel_error\": dark_coord_kernel_error,\n            \"dark_coord_commutator_violation\": dark_coord_commutator_violation,\n        },\n    )\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 22. Gate: generic access rank.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef gate_access_rank_generic() -> GateResult:\n    inv_sqrt2 = 1.0 / math.sqrt(2.0)\n\n    # 2x2: same average probability, different rank.\n    L_loss_2 = diag_matrix([1.0, 0.0])\n    L_full_2 = diag_matrix([inv_sqrt2, inv_sqrt2])\n\n    M_loss_2, eigs_loss_2, rank_loss_2, kappa_loss_2, pbar_loss_2 = access_metrics(L_loss_2)\n    M_full_2, eigs_full_2, rank_full_2, kappa_full_2, pbar_full_2 = access_metrics(L_full_2)\n\n    _check(_close(pbar_loss_2, 0.5), \"2x2 loss p_bar should be 0.5\")\n    _check(_close(pbar_full_2, 0.5), \"2x2 full p_bar should be 0.5\")\n    _check(rank_loss_2 == 1, \"2x2 loss rank should be 1\")\n    _check(rank_full_2 == 2, \"2x2 full rank should be 2\")\n    _check(kappa_loss_2 < 1e-12, \"2x2 loss kappa should be 0\")\n    _check(kappa_full_2 > 1.0 - 1e-12, \"2x2 full kappa should be 1\")\n\n    # Gate: eta semantics.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef gate_eta_semantics() -> GateResult:\n    eta_max_common_declared = EtaValue(\n        0.48,\n        EtaSemantics.MAX_TOLERANCE,\n        \"corpus-reported common max tolerance (not measured here)\",\n    )\n\n    raised = _expect_raises(\n        ValueError,\n        lambda: binary_recoverability_bound(eta_max_common_declared),\n        \"MAX_TOLERANCE must not be usable as recoverability input\",\n    )\n    _check(raised)\n\n    eta_noise = EtaValue(\n        0.01,\n        EtaSemantics.REALIZED_NOISE,\n        \"example realized cycle noise\",\n    )\n    bound_noise = binary_recoverability_bound(eta_noise)\n    _check(math.isclose(bound_noise, 0.27, rel_tol=1e-15, abs_tol=1e-15))\n\n    eta_rec = EtaValue(\n        0.02,\n        EtaSemantics.RECOVERABILITY_INPUT,\n        \"explicit recoverability input\",\n    )\n    bound_rec = binary_recoverability_bound(eta_rec)\n    _check(math.isclose(bound_rec, 0.29, rel_tol=1e-15, abs_tol=1e-15))\n\n    return GateResult(\n        name=\"eta_semantics\",\n        passed=True,\n        status=FINAL_STATUS[\"eta_type_separation\"],\n        details={\n            \"max_tolerance_as_recoverability_blocked\": raised,\n            \"bound_eta_noise_0p01\": bound_noise,\n            \"bound_eta_rec_0p02\": bound_rec,\n            \"eta_max_measurement_status\": FINAL_STATUS[\"eta_max_measurement\"],\n        },\n    )\n\n\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# 24. Gate: internal E_merge cross-audit.\n#     Validates the optimization pipeline, not corpus replication.\n# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef gate_emerge_internal() -> GateResult:\n    if not (HAVE_SCIPY and HAVE_NUMPY):\n        return GateResult(\n            name=\"emerge_internal\",\n            passed=False,\n
